92// if constexpr is C++17+; C++14 has relaxed constexpr but not if constexpr.
93#if __cplusplus >= 201703L
94#define LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR LUMEX_CONSTEXPR
95#elif __cplusplus >= 201402L
96#define LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR LUMEX_CONSTEXPR
97#else
98#define LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR inline
99#endif
100
101// std::array::at() is not constexpr until C++17; constexpr lookup tables need
102// operator[] on C++11/14.
103#if __cplusplus >= 201703L
104#define LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF(table, index) \
105 (table).at (static_cast<std::size_t> (index))
106#else
107#define LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF(table, index) \
108 (table)[static_cast<std::size_t> (index)]
109#endif
110
111LUMEX_CONST_NUM int kMaxCrcBitWidth = 64;
112LUMEX_CONST_NUM int kBitsPerByte = 8;
113LUMEX_CONST_NUM std::size_t kTableByteCount = 256U;
114LUMEX_CONST_NUM std::uint8_t kByteMaskU8 = 0xFFU;
115
116#if __cplusplus >= 201402L
117template <typename Spec>
118LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_FUNCTION void
119ValidateSpec () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
120{
121 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Spec::kWidth > 0 && Spec::kWidth <= kMaxCrcBitWidth,
122 "Spec::kWidth must be 1..64");
123 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
124 std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::is_integer,
125 "Spec::ValueType must be an integer type");
126 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
127 std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::digits >= Spec::kWidth,
128 "Spec::ValueType must hold at least kWidth bits");
129}
130#else
131template <typename Spec>
132inline void
133ValidateSpec () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
134{
135 struct validator_t
136 {
137 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Spec::kWidth > 0
138 && Spec::kWidth <= kMaxCrcBitWidth,
139 "Spec::kWidth must be 1..64");
140 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
141 std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::is_integer,
142 "Spec::ValueType must be an integer type");
143 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
144 std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::digits >= Spec::kWidth,
145 "Spec::ValueType must hold at least kWidth bits");
146 };
147 (void)sizeof (validator_t);
148}
149#endif
150
151// Partial specialization: avoid (1u64 << Width) when Width == digits(uint64_t)
152// (UB / -Wshift).
153template <int Width, typename Enable = void> struct mask_impl_t
154{
155 static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
156 Value () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
157 {
158 return (std::uint64_t{ 1 } << static_cast<unsigned> (Width))
159 - std::uint64_t{ 1 };
160 }
161};
162
163template <int Width>
164struct mask_impl_t<
165 Width,
166 typename std::enable_if<
167 Width == std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::digits, void>::type>
168{
169 static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
170 Value () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
171 {
172 return ~std::uint64_t{};
173 }
174};
175
176template <int Width>
177LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
178Mask () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
179{
180 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Width > 0 && Width <= kMaxCrcBitWidth,
181 "CRC width must be in 1..64");
182 return mask_impl_t<Width>::Value ();
183}
184
185// NOLINTBEGIN(bugprone-easily-swappable-parameters) - signatures match RevEng
186// notation (data, width / index, poly)
187LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
188Reflect (std::uint64_t data, int width) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
189{
190 std::uint64_t reflected = 0;
191 for (int bitIndex = 0; bitIndex < width; ++bitIndex)
192 if (((data >> bitIndex) & 1U) != 0U)
193 reflected |= (std::uint64_t{ 1 } << (width - 1 - bitIndex));
194 return reflected;
195}
196
197template <int Width>
198LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
199MsbTableByte (std::uint64_t tableIndex, std::uint64_t poly) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
200{
201 LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Width >= kBitsPerByte,
202 "MSB table entries assume width >= 8");
203 std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Width> ();
204 std::uint64_t const highBitMask = std::uint64_t{ 1 } << (Width - 1);
205 std::uint64_t registerValue = (tableIndex << (Width - kBitsPerByte)) & mask;
206 for (int step = 0; step < kBitsPerByte; ++step)
207 if ((registerValue & highBitMask) != 0U)
208 registerValue = ((registerValue << 1) & mask) ^ poly;
209 else
210 registerValue = (registerValue << 1) & mask;
211 return registerValue & mask;
212}
213
214template <int Width>
215LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
216LsbTableByte (std::uint64_t tableIndex, std::uint64_t poly) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
217{
218 std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Width> ();
219 std::uint64_t const reflectedPoly = Reflect (poly, Width) & mask;
220 std::uint64_t registerValue = tableIndex;
221 for (int step = 0; step < kBitsPerByte; ++step)
222 if ((registerValue & 1U) != 0U)
223 registerValue = (registerValue >> 1) ^ reflectedPoly;
224 else
225 registerValue >>= 1;
226 return registerValue & mask;
227}
228
229// NOLINTEND(bugprone-easily-swappable-parameters)
230
231// C++14: non-const std::array::operator[]/at are not constexpr; build the
232// table via index_sequence
233// and aggregate initialization instead of a mutating loop inside
234// constexpr MakeLookupTable.
235#if __cplusplus >= 201402L
236template <typename Spec, std::size_t Index>
237LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_FUNCTION std::uint64_t
238LookupTableEntry () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
239{
240 return Spec::kRefIn
241 ? LsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (static_cast<std::uint64_t> (Index),
242 Spec::kPoly)
243 : MsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (static_cast<std::uint64_t> (Index),
244 Spec::kPoly);
245}
246
247template <typename Spec, std::size_t... I>
248LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_FUNCTION std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount>
249MakeLookupTableImpl (std::index_sequence<I...>) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
250{
251 return std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount>{
252 { LookupTableEntry<Spec, I> ()... }
253 };
254}
255#endif
256
257template <typename Spec>
258LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount>
259MakeLookupTable () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
260{
261 ValidateSpec<Spec> ();
262#if __cplusplus >= 201402L
263 return MakeLookupTableImpl<Spec> (
264 std::make_index_sequence<kTableByteCount>{});
265#else
266 std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount> lookupTable{};
267 for (int tableIndex = 0; tableIndex < static_cast<int> (kTableByteCount);
268 ++tableIndex)
269 {
270 std::size_t const entry = static_cast<std::size_t> (tableIndex);
271 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefIn)
272 LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (lookupTable, entry) = LsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (
273 static_cast<std::uint64_t> (tableIndex), Spec::kPoly);
274 else LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (lookupTable, entry)
275 = MsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (
276 static_cast<std::uint64_t> (tableIndex), Spec::kPoly);
277 }
278 return lookupTable;
279#endif
280}
281
282template <typename Spec>
283LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
284ComputeBitwise (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
285{
286 ValidateSpec<Spec> ();
287 std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Spec::kWidth> ();
288 std::uint64_t const polyMasked = Spec::kPoly & mask;
289 std::uint64_t const polyReflected
290 = Reflect (polyMasked, Spec::kWidth) & mask;
291 std::uint64_t crcRegister
292 = (Spec::kRefIn ? Reflect (Spec::kInit, Spec::kWidth) : Spec::kInit)
293 & mask;
294
295 if (data == nullptr || size == 0UL)
296 return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (0);
297
298 // NOLINTBEGIN(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
299 for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size; ++offset)
300 {
301 std::uint8_t const messageByte = data[offset];
302 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefIn)
303 {
304 for (int bitIndex = 0; bitIndex < kBitsPerByte; ++bitIndex)
305 {
306 std::uint8_t const dataBit = static_cast<std::uint8_t> (
307 (static_cast<unsigned> (messageByte) >> bitIndex) & 1U);
308 crcRegister ^= static_cast<std::uint64_t> (dataBit);
309 if ((crcRegister & 1U) != 0U)
310 crcRegister = (crcRegister >> 1) ^ polyReflected;
311 else
312 crcRegister >>= 1;
313 crcRegister &= mask;
314 }
315 }
316 else
317 {
318 int const msbShiftIndex = Spec::kWidth - 1;
319 for (int bitIndex = 0; bitIndex < kBitsPerByte; ++bitIndex)
320 {
321 std::uint8_t const dataBit = static_cast<std::uint8_t> (
322 (static_cast<unsigned> (messageByte)
323 >> (kBitsPerByte - 1 - bitIndex))
324 & 1U);
325 std::uint64_t const topBit = (crcRegister >> msbShiftIndex) & 1U;
326 crcRegister = (crcRegister << 1) & mask;
327 if ((topBit ^ static_cast<std::uint64_t> (dataBit)) != 0U)
328 crcRegister ^= polyMasked;
329 }
330 }
331 }
332 // NOLINTEND(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
333
334 // RefIn=true: same as zlib / RevEng tables - no final Reflect before xorout.
335 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (!Spec::kRefIn && Spec::kRefOut)
336 crcRegister = Reflect (crcRegister, Spec::kWidth) & mask;
337 crcRegister = (crcRegister ^ Spec::kXorOut) & mask;
338 return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (crcRegister);
339}
340
341template <typename Spec>
342LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
343ComputeTableDriven (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
344{
345 ValidateSpec<Spec> ();
346#if __cplusplus >= 201402L
347 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount> kByteLookupTable
348 = MakeLookupTable<Spec> ();
349#else
350 static std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount> const kByteLookupTable
351 = MakeLookupTable<Spec> ();
352#endif
353 std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Spec::kWidth> ();
354 std::uint64_t crcRegister
355 = (Spec::kRefIn ? Reflect (Spec::kInit, Spec::kWidth) : Spec::kInit)
356 & mask;
357
358 if (data == nullptr || size == 0UL)
359 return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (0);
360
361 // NOLINTBEGIN(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
362 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefIn)
363 {
364 for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size; ++offset)
365 {
366 std::uint8_t const messageByte = data[offset];
367 crcRegister
368 = (crcRegister >> kBitsPerByte)
369 ^ LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (
370 kByteLookupTable,
371 (crcRegister ^ static_cast<std::uint64_t> (messageByte))
372 & kByteMaskU8);
373 crcRegister &= mask;
374 }
375 }
376 else
377 {
378 for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size; ++offset)
379 {
380 std::uint8_t const messageByte = data[offset];
381 crcRegister = ((crcRegister << kBitsPerByte) & mask)
382 ^ LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (
383 kByteLookupTable,
384 ((crcRegister >> (Spec::kWidth - kBitsPerByte))
385 ^ static_cast<std::uint64_t> (messageByte))
386 & kByteMaskU8);
387 crcRegister &= mask;
388 }
389 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefOut)
390 crcRegister = Reflect (crcRegister, Spec::kWidth) & mask;
391 }
392 // NOLINTEND(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
393
394 crcRegister = (crcRegister ^ Spec::kXorOut) & mask;
395 return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (crcRegister);
396}
397
398template <typename Spec, typename Enable = void> struct crc_dispatch_t;
399
400template <typename Spec>
401struct crc_dispatch_t<
402 Spec, typename std::enable_if<(Spec::kWidth < kBitsPerByte), void>::type>
403{
404 static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
405 Run (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
406 {
407 return ComputeBitwise<Spec> (data, size);
408 }
409};
410
411template <typename Spec>
412struct crc_dispatch_t<
413 Spec, typename std::enable_if<(Spec::kWidth >= kBitsPerByte), void>::type>
414{
415 static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
416 Run (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
417 {
418 return ComputeTableDriven<Spec> (data, size);
419 }
420};
421
422template <typename Spec>
423LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
424Compute (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
425{
426 return crc_dispatch_t<Spec>::Run (data, size);
427}

2339// CRC-82/DARC (width=82) is not in this catalogue: its polynomial does not
2340// fit in std::uint64_t, and kMaxCrcBitWidth is 64. Every other RevEng entry
2341// is a specification here and a named engine after CrcParametric.
2342

2416// NOLINTEND(readability-identifier-naming)
2417
2423template <typename Spec> class CrcParametric
2424{
2425public:
2426 using ValueType = typename Spec::ValueType;
2427
2428 CrcParametric () = delete;
2429 CrcParametric (CrcParametric const &) = delete;
2430 CrcParametric (CrcParametric &&) = delete;
2431 CrcParametric &operator= (CrcParametric const &) = delete;
2432 CrcParametric &operator= (CrcParametric &&) = delete;
2433 ~CrcParametric () = default;
2434
2439 LUMEX_ATTRIBUTE_NODISCARD (
2440 "CRC result is required for integrity verification.")
2441 static ValueType
2442 calculate (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
2443 {
2444 return Detail::Compute<Spec> (data, size);
2445 }
2446
2447 LUMEX_ATTRIBUTE_NODISCARD (
2448 "CRC result is required for integrity verification.")
2449 static ValueType
2450 calculate (std::vector<std::uint8_t> const &data) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
2451 {
2452 if (data.empty ())
2453 return Detail::Compute<Spec> (nullptr, 0);
2454 return Detail::Compute<Spec> (data.data (), data.size ());
2455 }
2456
2457#if __cplusplus >= 202002L
2458 LUMEX_ATTRIBUTE_NODISCARD (
2459 "CRC result is required for integrity verification.")
2460 static ValueType
2461 calculate (std::span<std::uint8_t const> data) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
2462 {
2463 return Detail::Compute<Spec> (data.data (), data.size ());
2464 }
2465#endif
2466};
